            - Science: The Celts have completed 2 spaceship parts (33%) and have Apollo complete. I have Apollo complete but 0 parts. Brazil, Ethiopia, Germany, Rome, Inca also have 0. The Celts are the clear contender.
            - Cultural: I'm influential over 2 civs (Germany, Brazil). Need to influence everyone. The Celts are influential over 4 civs and are the contender. I'm close to influencing Brazil (6 turns), Ethiopia (38 turns), Inca (42 turns). But The Celts are a problem - they're at 62% and rising.
            - Diplomatic: Votes needed: 42. The Celts have 22 delegates (52%), I have 19 (45%). World Congress votes in 6 turns. This is very close! The Celts are the contender but I'm not far behind.
            
            **Wars:**
            - I'm at war with The Celts (our score: -6%, war weariness: 22%)
            - I'm at war with Germany (our score: 32%, war weariness: 17%)
            - I'm at war with Ethiopia (our score: 100%, war weariness: 18%)
            - I'm denounced by The Inca
            
            **Key Relationships:**
            - Friendly with Rome, Brazil
            - At war with Celts, Germany, Ethiopia
            - Hostile with Inca (denounced)
            
            **My Civ Stats:**
            - Score: 5646 (highest in game!)
            - Era: Information
            - Technologies: 69
            - Military Strength: 133,241 (very high)
            - Science: 2418/turn
            - Culture: 2159/turn
            - Tourism: 847.5/turn
            - Gold: 9292, +528/turn
            - Cities: 11
            
            **The Celts Stats:**
            - Score: 5386
            - Era: Information

            - Military Strength: 26,480 (much lower than mine)
            - Cities: 27
            - Tourism: 3268/turn (massive!)
            - Culture: 4688/turn
            
            **Strategic Assessment:**
            
            1. **Science Victory:** The Celts are ahead with 2 parts complete. I have 0. Catching up seems difficult given they also have more techs.
            
            2. **Cultural Victory:** I'm influential over 2 civs, close on several others, but The Celts are at 4 and rapidly approaching influential on Brazil (8 turns) and me (52 turns). Their tourism is 4x mine. This is very hard to win.
            
            3. **Diplomatic Victory:** This seems most viable! I have 19 votes, need 42. The Celts have 22. World Congress votes in 6 turns. If I can gain 3 more votes than the Celts in these 6 turns, I could potentially win. I have spheres of influence on Hanoi, Panama City, and Utica is my ally. I need to secure more city-state allies and potentially get voting bonuses.
            
            4. **Domination:** Unlikely given I need 8 capitals and The Celts already have 5.
            
            Given the diplomatic vote is in 6 turns, and I'm very close in delegate count to The Celts, I should double down on Diplomatic victory (UnitedNations grand strategy).
            
            My current strategy already has UnitedNations as grand strategy, with high flavors for Diplomacy (100), Gold (100), Science (100), etc.

Globalization is important for diplomatic victory (+1 delegate from trade routes?), and more importantly, the UN requires certain techs. Actually, let me think... Telecommunications allows Interpretive Center and leads to Robotics and The Internet. Alternative Energy allows Solar Plant and Recycling Center and leads to Robotics. Mobile Tactics leads to Stealth.
            
            For diplomatic victory, I probably want to get to Globalization if it helps with delegates. But looking at the tech tree in Civ V, Globalization usually gives an extra delegate from Diplomats or trade routes. Lasers → Globalization is a valid path. Actually I need to check: the tech listed says "Completing it would unlock: Particle Physics, Globalization, Nuclear Fusion". So Lasers is a prerequisite for Globalization. Given the diplomatic win is close and the vote is in 6 turns, getting Globalization before the vote would be huge. I should keep Lasers.

Looking at ActiveResolutions: "World Ideology: Order" was enacted on turn 439 and says "Enables the Diplomatic Victory if the United Nations has been constructed." Wait, has the UN been constructed? It says "Available once any Civilization discovers Nuclear Fission." And it was enacted on 439. But there are proposals now, not a World Leader election. Let me re-read...
            
            Actually in Vox Populi, the Diplomatic Victory works through accumulating delegates and getting elected World Leader. The vote happens periodically. "VotesNeeded: 42. Status: World Congress - Voting in 6 turns". So this is a regular World Congress session, not necessarily the World Leader vote.             Regardless, my best path is still Diplomatic - accumulating delegates for when the World Leader election does happen. And even regular World Congress votes matter for resolutions that can help me.

            So The Celts are close to winning in multiple victory types! They could win:
